The system integrates six main modules (alignment, distance, similarity, search, generation metrics, and BLAST homology search), operating at character, word, token, line, and residue levels. Its C++-based algorithms compile to WebAssembly, so core operations run locally by default without any installation or data upload. The interface reports scores with their "evidence" (alignments, edit paths, metric matches, search hits, and homology traces), making methods inspectable, debuggable, and comparable on shared inputs. Internal benchmarks show speedups of up to 2,500x over the Python predecessor, faster global/local alignment than a general-purpose native C aligner, and exact agreement with independent references under declared settings. For homology search, the scoped client-side blastn path closely matches NCBI BLAST+ rankings and statistics under matched parameters. A curated showcase and Learn mode present canonical algorithms and metrics as reusable demonstrations. string2string Studio is open-source and freely available at string2string.org.

</details>


### [208] [When Attention Goes Blind: Numerical Failure in ALiBi Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03994)
*Christopher Schröder,Lukas Gienapp,Ferdinand Schlatt,Martin Potthast,Gerhard Heyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了ALiBi位置编码中线性偏差缩放导致的浮点精度下溢问题，指出这会使得大量注意力权重归零，导致部分注意力头“失明”。通过实验验证了该现象在预训练模型中的存在，并提出了四种缓解策略，其中对数缩放距离效果最佳。尽管存在此缺陷，默认ALiBi斜率仍是强有力的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并分析ALiBi位置编码中被忽视的失败模式（浮点精度下溢），评估其对模型性能的影响，并提出有效的缓解策略以优化基于ALiBi的模型训练。

Method: 1. 理论分析ALiBi线性偏差缩放导致的浮点下溢机制；2. 在1.48亿参数的解码器模型上进行大规模预训练实验，分离该问题与上下文退化效应；3. 提出并评估四种训练时的缓解策略（包括对数缩放距离）；4. 使用钥匙检索（passkey retrieval）和针在 haystack 中检索（needle-in-a-haystack）等基准测试评估性能。

Result: 1. ALiBi的下溢问题会显著损害令牌检索能力，但对标准解码器基准测试影响较小；2. 对数缩放距离提供了最一致的改进效果；3. 默认ALiBi斜率在针在haystack检索任务中表现依然强劲；4. 确认了该失败模式在现有SOTA预训练模型中的普遍性。

Conclusion: 虽然ALiBi存在浮点下溢导致的注意力头失效问题，但通过采用特定的缓解策略（如对数缩放距离）可以有效改善关键检索任务的性能。研究结果为如何更好地训练使用ALiBi的模型提供了具体建议，并肯定了其在特定场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We identify a previously overlooked failure mode of ALiBi positional encoding: its linear bias scaling underflows floating-point precision, which zeroes out a large fraction of attention weights and renders the affected attention heads partially blind. We analyze this failure mode, characterize its impact, and examine four mitigation strategies. We further demonstrate its occurrence in state-of-the-art pretrained models based on ALiBi. Comprehensive pretraining experiments with 148M-parameter decoder models help us to disentangle its effects from out-of-context degradation. We find that ALiBi's failure mode can substantially impair token retrieval while having only a minor effect on standard decoder benchmarks. We propose four training-time mitigation strategies and evaluate them individually and in combinations, finding that log-scaled distances yield the most consistent improvements in passkey retrieval. Despite this problem, default ALiBi slopes remain a surprisingly strong baseline, particularly for needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. Based on these findings we provide concrete recommendations on how to train models with ALiBi.

</details>


### [209] [PAST-Bench: Benchmarking the Foundations of Recursive Self-Improvement in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04003)
*Shuhan Xue,Zixin Ding,Yichen Shen,Yinjie Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Yuxin Chen,Mengdi Wang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PAST-Bench基准测试，系统评估个人AI代理利用保留经验进行递归自我改进的能力，并据此开发Hermes+框架以提升改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管个人AI代理具备跨会话保留偏好、任务和技能的能力，但现有研究缺乏系统性测试来验证保留的经验是否真正转化为未来的行为改进。

Method: 构建PAST-Bench基准，涵盖26个场景和204个回合，通过控制变量隔离“保留经验”的影响；评估七种基础模型和四种代理框架在记忆、程序复用等方面的表现；基于诊断结果设计Hermes+，引入五项针对代理循环阶段的干预措施。

Result: 经验确实带来改进但不均衡；相同总体增益的代理在支持路径的证据上差异显著；Hermes+提高了平均增益，尤其在替换过时状态的任务中效果最强，但效果仍依赖具体能力和模型。

Conclusion: PAST-Bench和Hermes+为研究持久性代理如何从单纯保留经验进化到系统性利用经验改进提供了评估与诊断基础。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires agents to turn accumulated experience into better future behavior. Personal AI agents offer a concrete setting for studying this capability because they retain preferences, task histories, tool routines, and learned skills across sessions. Yet whether retained experience actually improves them over time has not been systematically tested. We introduce PAST-Bench, a benchmark designed to isolate this question. Each agent runs through ordered sequences of fresh-session tasks under matched conditions that turn retained experience on and off. It spans 26 scenarios and 204 episodes across memory, procedural reuse, information gathering, and update. We report both later-task gains and whether those gains follow the intended save, retrieve, and update pathway. Across seven base models and four agent frameworks, improvement is real but uneven across capabilities. Agents with the same headline gain can differ markedly in whether that gain is supported by evidence of the intended pathway. Guided by these findings, we develop Hermes+, which extends Hermes with five targeted interventions across stages of the agent loop. Hermes+ raises the average gain from retained experience and provides clearer pathway evidence, with its strongest improvement on tasks requiring outdated state to be replaced, although the effect remains capability- and model-dependent. Together, PAST-Bench and Hermes+ provide an evaluation and diagnostic foundation for studying how persistent agents can progress from retaining experience to systematically improving through it. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/PAST-Bench

</details>


### [210] [TurnSight: Turn-Level Hindsight Self-Distillation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04007)
*Changle Qu,Sunhao Dai,Hengyi Cai,Yuqi Zhou,Xinran Chen,Simon,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TurnSight，一种基于回合级回溯自蒸馏的框架，通过执行条件回溯和多视界交叉验证生成更精准的强化学习信号，解决长周期工具交互中的细粒度信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖轨迹级监督，导致在长周期工具推理（TIR）中细粒度信用分配困难；传统自蒸馏方法使用的特权上下文（如标准答案或检索技能）与实际智能体访问状态不符，且词元级监督无法捕捉工具交互的回合级结构。

Method: 提出TurnSight框架：1) 从执行条件的回溯中直接获取监督信号；2) 构建具有不同前瞻视界的多个回溯视图，并通过跨视界方向一致性选择可靠监督；3) 在兄弟 rollout 间归一化该信号，自适应调节强化学习的优势函数，同时保持原始优化方向。

Result: 在三个基准测试上进行了广泛实验，证明了TurnSight的有效性。

Conclusion: TurnSight通过回合级回溯自蒸馏机制，有效提升了大语言模型在复杂工具交互任务中的表现，解决了现有方法在信用分配和上下文真实性方面的局限。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables LLMs to solve complex tasks through iterative tool interactions. However, existing reinforcement learning methods often rely on trajectory-level supervision, limiting fine-grained credit assignment in long-horizon TIR scenarios. On-policy self-distillation offers denser signals through teacher branches with privileged context, but existing approaches typically derive such context from ground-truth answers or retrieved skills, which may not reflect the states actually visited by the agent. Moreover, token-level supervision fails to capture the turn-level structure of tool interactions. To address this, we propose TurnSight, a turn-level hindsight self-distillation framework that derives supervision directly from execution-conditioned hindsight. It then constructs multiple hindsight views with different lookahead horizons and selects reliable supervision through cross-horizon directional agreement. Finally, the selected hindsight signal is normalized across sibling rollouts and used to adaptively modulate RL advantages while preserving their original optimization direction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of TurnSight. Our codes are available at https://github.com/quchangle1/TurnSight.

</details>


### [211] [WorldCup Arena: Prospective, Leakage-Free Evaluation of Frontier LLMs on a Live Tournament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04008)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大型语言模型（LLM）预测能力的非回顾性基准测试。在2026年世界杯期间，评估了六个前沿LLM的实时比赛预测能力。结果显示，这些模型的平均准确率为63.9%，与跟随博彩公司热门选择持平；模型间一致性高但准确性并未显著提升，且对势均力敌的比赛预测能力较差。该研究揭示了当前前沿LLM在实时动态预测任务中表现趋同，未展现出显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM预测能力基准测试多为回顾性设计，面临记忆化（memorisation）和数据泄露的挑战。为了构建一个无数据泄露、由构造而非过滤保证真实性的评估环境，作者设计了基于实时事件的前瞻性基准测试。

Method: 在2026年国际足联世界杯进行的39天内，对六个具有扩展思维能力和原生服务端网络搜索功能的前沿LLM进行评估。在比赛开始前，要求模型为所有104场比赛的七个市场、12个小组冠军以及赛前总池进行预测。最终收集并记录了4,494个评分预测结果。

Result: 1. 平均准确率：模型在单场胜负预测上的平均准确率为63.9%，与盲目跟随博彩公司热门选项的表现相当。
2. 行为特征：模型之间的一致性远高于其正确率，因此多数投票无法提升效果；模型倾向于低估平局和进球数，且比分预测高度集中在单一典型结果上。
3. 影响因素：准确率取决于比赛的悬殊程度而非信息丰富度；在信息最丰富但竞争最激烈的比赛中，准确率反而下降；而对整个赛事结果的预测表现较好。
4. 模型差异：当前一代前沿系统在任务上没有明显区分度，排名稳定，中间波动小，差距始终狭窄。

Conclusion: 当前前沿大语言模型在实时体育预测任务中表现出高度的行为趋同，未能通过复杂推理或信息整合显著超越简单的启发式策略（如跟随热门选项）。该研究发布了一个包含简报、赛程、官方结果及评分代码的全新基准数据集，为未来LLM实时预测能力的评估提供了无泄露的标准框架。

Abstract: Benchmarks that measure the forecasting ability of large language models are almost always retrospective: the event has happened, the answer is somewhere on the Web, and the evaluation must defend itself against memorisation. We report the opposite design. Over the 39 days of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, six frontier LLMs -- all with extended thinking and native server-side web search -- were asked before every kickoff, one match at a time, to fill in a seven-market prediction card for all 104 matches, plus 12 group winners and a pre-tournament outright pool; no answer existed when the question was asked, so the evaluation is leakage-free by construction rather than by filtering, and the frozen archive holds 4,494 scored predictions. What the tournament establishes is a set of behaviours the six systems share. On match outcome they average 63.9%, level with backing the bookmaker's favourite -- which is in fact what they usually do. They agree with one another far more often than they are right, so a majority vote adds nothing. They under-commit to draws and to goals, and crowd their scoreline picks onto a single prototypical result. Accuracy tracks how lopsided a fixture is rather than how much is known about it: it collapses in the closest ties, where the dossiers are richest, while questions about the tournament as a whole are answered well. On this task the current generation of frontier systems is not sharply differentiated: the standings hold up at the top and the bottom across the run and churn in the middle, and the margins stay narrow throughout. The briefing dossiers, fixtures and official results are released as a benchmark, together with the scoring code.

</details>


### [212] [SocietyBench: Forecasting Counterfactual Social-World Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04009)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SocietyBench，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）社会事件理解与预测能力的端到端基准测试。该基准通过构建反事实时间线来防止模型利用预训练记忆作弊，并在概率校准和时间准确性两个维度上进行评分。实验显示，即使是前沿LLM在此任务上的表现也有限（最高75.0/100），且不同模型框架并未带来显著提升，突显了当前LLM在社会动态预测方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型及其代理主要侧重于任务完成能力（如修复bug、操作GUI），而缺乏对模型理解和预测真实社会事件发展轨迹这一互补性社交能力的系统性评估。

Method: 提出SocietyBench基准：1) 收集五个平台上的新闻和社交媒体帖子；2) 构建包含事实事件和公众舆论层的时间线；3) 采用三阶段程序替换命名实体并偏移日期，将真实事件转化为结构相同但无表面标签的反事实世界，以消除预训练数据泄露的影响；4) 在概率校准和时间准确性两个正交维度上对预测问题进行评分。

Result: 在涵盖中英文版本的5个异质事件和125个预测点上，最强的六个前沿LLM得分仅为75.0/100（基准锚点为50）。模型在两个评分轴上表现分离（即可能校准强但时间弱，或反之）。基于同一基础模型的三个智能体框架未能超越基础模型，两种无模型启发式方法表现更差。单个轴上的事件间差距高达21.4分，证明了多事件评估的必要性。

Conclusion: 当前主流LLM在社会事件预测方面仍有显著局限，简单的智能体架构改进无法有效弥补这一差距。SocietyBench提供了一个严谨的评估框架，揭示了模型在剥离表面信息后对社会动态推理能力的不足，并公开了所有相关资源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), and the agents built on top of them, are now benchmarked heavily on whether they can finish a task -- fix a bug, drive a browser, operate a GUI. A complementary social ability, namely how well a model understands and forecasts the way real social events unfold, has barely been measured. We introduce SocietyBench, an end-to-end benchmark that takes a one-line event topic, collects Web news and social-media posts across five platforms, distills them into a date-indexed timeline that keeps factual events and a public-opinion layer separate, and then turns every cutoff date on that timeline into an audited bank of forecasting questions. Questions are scored on two orthogonal 100-point axes: probability calibration and temporal accuracy. Before any model sees a timeline, a three-phase procedure replaces every named entity and shifts every date by a per-event constant, turning a real arc into a counterfactual social world -- structurally identical to what happened, but stripped of the surface labels a model could match against pre-training memory. On five heterogeneous events and 125 prediction points in Chinese and English editions, the strongest of six frontier LLMs reaches only 75.0 out of 100, against a trivial anchor of 50. The two axes come apart: a model can be calibration-strong but time-weak, or the reverse. Three agent frameworks built on a shared base model fail to improve on that base, and two model-free heuristics trail every LLM. Per-event gaps reach 21.4 points on a single axis, which is our main argument for evaluating on several events rather than one. All anonymized timelines, question banks, ground truth, and scoring code are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Extracting ODRL Policies from Business Process Models: A Graph Traversal Approach to Compliance-by-Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02607)
*Meem Arafat Manab,Marinella Quaranta,Ilaria Angela Amantea,Sheyla Leyva-Sánchez,Víctor Rodríguez-Doncel*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种从BPMN模型自动提取ODRL策略的流水线，解决了政策基础设施无法访问流程模型中规范性内容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织维护大量BPMN流程模型，但其中编码的义务、许可和禁止等规范性内容对政策基础设施不可见；而手动编写ODRL策略成本高、耗时长，且大语言模型生成存在结构无效风险。

Method: 构建了一个自动化流水线，遍历BPMN过程图，通过可达性检查将任务分类为odrl:Duty或odrl:Permission；创新性地引入中间捕获事件作为带有提升约束的odrl:Prohibition规则，以捕捉先前方法忽略的等待语义。

Result: 实现了“基于提取的合规性”方法，直接从现有的流程模型中推导出可审计、互操作的ODRL策略，填补了BPMN与机器可读政策之间的空白。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了BPMN中规范性内容难以被政策基础设施利用的问题，提供了高效、准确的ODRL自动生成方案，并提供了开源实现和在线演示。

Abstract: Organisations maintain large corpora of process models expressed in the Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN), yet the normative content encoded in those models, the obligations, permissions, and prohibitions that govern participant behaviour, remains inaccessible to policy infrastructure. The Open Digital Rights Language (ODRL) is the emerging lingua franca of machine-readable policy, but authoring ODRL at scale is slow, expert-intensive work, and generation by large language models introduces well-documented risks of structural invalidity. We present a pipeline that resolves this gap by extracting ODRL policies automatically from BPMN XML, grounded in the observation that BPMN control flow encodes deontic modalities by construction. The pipeline traverses the BPMN process graph, classifies each task as an odrl:Duty or odrl:Permission via a reachability check, and introduces a novel treatment of intermediate catch events as odrl:Prohibition rules with lifting constraints, capturing waiting semantics that prior approaches have dropped. The result is a compliance-by-extraction approach in which auditable, interoperable ODRL policies are derived directly from the process models organisations already maintain. The implementation and a live demonstration are available at https://github.com/manabcodes/bpmn2odrl and https://bpmn.linkeddata.es/.

</details>


### [214] [Scalable Exact Densest P-Partite Subgraph Search in Heterogeneous Information Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03061)
*Jiadong Xie,Jiaming Yang,Kangfei Zhao,Jeffrey Xu Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出BoxDPpS算法，通过盒级搜索、安全区域剪枝、消除冗余表示和压缩辅助网络等技术，显著提升了异构信息网络中最密P部子图搜索（DPpS）问题的求解效率，在保持精确最优解的同时实现了平均27.04倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DPpS精确方法受限于候选iRM-set数量庞大以及反复求解大型辅助网络的高成本，导致可扩展性不足。

Method: 提出BoxDPpS高效精确方法，包括：1) 进行盒级搜索并应用安全区域剪枝；2) 消除同一iRM-set的冗余表示；3) 通过有界预热改进早期剪枝；4) 压缩每个固定M的辅助网络以进行精确参数伪流求解。

Result: 在七个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，BoxDPpS保留了精确的DPpS最优解，相比最先进的方法平均加速27.04倍。

Conclusion: BoxDPpS有效解决了现有DPpS方法的性能瓶颈，是一种既精确又高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous information networks (HINs) model typed entities and typed relations, where dense cross-type structures can reveal cohesive semantic patterns such as prolific author-paper-venue groups. Given a query meta-path, the densest P-partite subgraph search (DPpS) problem jointly selects a nonempty vertex set at each typed position and maximizes the number of induced meta-path instances normalized by the geometric mean of the selected set sizes. Existing exact methods solve DPpS by searching over iRM-sets and reducing each fixed-M problem to minimum-cut computations. However, their scalability is limited by the large number of candidate iRM-sets and the high cost of repeatedly solving large auxiliary networks. In this paper, we propose BoxDPpS, an efficient exact approach that reduces both sources of cost. It performs box-level search with safe region pruning, eliminates redundant representations of the same iRM-set, improves early pruning through bounded warm-up, and compresses each fixed-M auxiliary network for exact parametric pseudoflow solving. Experiments on seven real-world datasets show that BoxDPpS preserves the exact DPpS optimum while achieving an average speedup of 27.04x over the state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [215] [SieveIVF: Threshold-Aware IVF Execution for Large-Scale Training Data Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03199)
*Zhisheng Hu,Zhifang Li,Junjie Chen,Ke Xu,Yuxuan Li,Chufeng Chen,Rui Chen,Zhe Chen,Ming-Chang Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出SieveIVF，一种感知阈值的IVF执行器，通过早期停止机制优化基于嵌入的训练数据去重，在保持召回率的同时显著提升搜索速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定探测倒排文件（IVF）搜索为每个查询分配相同的分区预算，忽略了应用层面的相似度阈值谓词，导致计算资源浪费，尽管合格邻居往往在搜索早期出现且深度变化剧烈。

Method: 设计SieveIVF执行器，当连续W次搜索未找到合格候选者时停止；结合持续批处理按分区分组就绪查询，并引入前瞻调度器仅暴露由停止规则承诺的工作，以增加并发度而不改变停止决策或返回结果。

Result: 在Lance中实现SieveIVF，当W=8时，在四个10M Hunyuan工作负载上比固定探测IVF快4.1-7.6倍，在两个100M公开工作负载上快6.1-8.4倍，召回率损失极小（Hunyuan为0.03-1.13个百分点，公开数据集为1.43-2.29个百分点）。

Conclusion: 应用程序谓词可以有效指导IVF的工作分配，无需更改索引结构或有界top-k接口即可实现性能大幅提升。

Abstract: Embedding-based training data deduplication retrieves candidate duplicate edges above an application similarity threshold, but fixed-probe inverted-file (IVF) search ignores this predicate when giving every query the same partition budget. Across four Hunyuan workloads, qualifying neighbors appear early despite sharply varying search depths. We present SieveIVF, a threshold-aware IVF executor that stops after $W$ consecutive searches find no qualifying candidate. The systems challenge is to preserve partition-major batching when each query's remaining work depends on prior results. Continuous batching groups ready queries by partition. A lookahead scheduler layers on top, exposing only work committed by the stopping rule to increase concurrency without changing stopping decisions or returned results. We implement SieveIVF in Lance. At $W=8$, SieveIVF is $4.1$--$7.6\times$ faster than fixed-probe IVF on four 10M Hunyuan workloads and $6.1$--$8.4\times$ faster on two public 100M workloads under the same index and search parameters, with pooled filtered top-10 recall losses of $0.03$--$1.13$ percentage points on Hunyuan and $1.43$--$2.29$ percentage points on the public workloads. These results show how an application predicate can guide IVF work allocation without changing the index or bounded top-$k$ interface.

</details>


### [216] [Getting to the Root: A Combined Complexity Perspective on Consistent Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03477)
*Miika Hannula*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了在特定假设下（布尔自连接无关合取查询、主键为单属性、攻击图无环），一致查询回答问题的组合复杂度。主要发现：当键-非键图中源强连通分量数量有界时，问题可在多项式时间内解决；否则为Pi_2^P完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去二十年主要研究一致查询回答的数据复杂度，而对其组合复杂度关注较少。旨在隔离使该计算问题变得可处理的查询特定结构特征。

Method: 聚焦于主键约束下的布尔自连接无关合取查询，且限制攻击图为无环（即存在一阶重写）。引入“键-非键图”概念，并开发变量守卫、攻击传播图、数据库与查询饱和及查询规范化等新技术。

Result: 证明了在上述假设下，若键-非键图中的源强连通分量（SCCs）数量受常数限制，则一致查询回答的组合复杂度属于多项式时间类；若无此限制，则该问题为Pi_2^P完全。

Conclusion: 通过限定查询结构和主键类型，明确了影响一致查询回答组合复杂度的关键因素，填补了该领域在组合复杂度方面的研究空白。

Abstract: The data complexity of consistent query answering has been extensively studied over the past two decades. In this paper, we study its combined complexity, which has gained less attention. The input is a triple consisting of a set of integrity constraints, a (potentially inconsistent) database, and a query, and the question is to determine whether all repairs of the database with respect to the constraints satisfy the query. Our aim is to isolate query-specific structural features that make this problem computationally tractable. We focus on Boolean self-join-free conjunctive queries over primary-key constraints, as in this context the corresponding data complexity problem has been completely classified. Moreover, we restrict attention to queries with an acyclic attack graph, which are precisely those queries that admit a first-order rewriting. As an additional simplifying assumption, we focus on unary primary keys. Each conjunctive query with unary primary keys naturally gives rise to a directed graph, which we call the key-nonkey graph, where each atom contributes an edge from its key variable to each of its non-key variables. Our main result is that, under the stated assumptions, the combined complexity of consistent query answering is in polynomial time whenever the number of source strongly connected components (SCCs) in the key-nonkey graph is bounded by a constant. To prove this result, we develop novel concepts, such as variable guarding and attack-propagation graphs, and techniques, such as database and query saturation and query normalization. In addition, without the boundedness restriction, the combined complexity of this problem is shown to be Pi_2^P-complete.

</details>


### [217] [RAG-Stack: Co-Optimizing RAG Serving Performance and Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03487)
*Haiqiang Zhang,Yuanqing Lei,Wanting Li,Tao Zhang,Wenqi Jiang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: RAG-Stack是一个用于高效发现检索增强生成（RAG）系统中质量与性能帕累托前沿的框架，通过算法、工作负载抽象和性能模型三个组件，在无需部署所有候选配置的情况下搜索最优设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG系统存在大量配置选择（如检索索引、模型选择、检索调用方式），每种配置在答案质量和推理性能之间有不同的权衡，导致难以针对特定应用部署选择最优配置。

Method: 提出RAG-Stack框架，包含三个核心组件：1) RAG-PE：迭代设计空间探索算法，用于选择下一个待评估的RAG配置；2) RAG-IR：面向多样化RAG算法的工作负载抽象；3) RAG-CM：预测给定硬件上最佳部署和服务性能的性能模型。

Result: 在相同优化迭代次数下，RAG-Stack在不同数据集上发现的帕累托前沿覆盖了比现有最先进配置搜索方法多52.5%至153.2%的归一化质量-性能空间。

Conclusion: RAG-Stack能够有效搜索联合算法-系统配置空间，并支持将现有帕累托前沿迁移到新服务系统，显著优于现有配置搜索方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which augments large language model (LLM) generation with information retrieved from databases, has become a widely used approach for knowledge-intensive applications. Modern RAG systems, however, expose many configuration choices, such as retrieval indexes, model selections, and how models invoke retrieval. Each configuration yields a different trade-off between answer quality and serving performance, making it challenging to choose the optimal setting for a specific application deployment. We present RAG-Stack, a framework for efficiently discovering quality-performance Pareto frontiers across diverse RAG applications and serving systems. RAG-Stack consists of RAG-PE, an iterative design-space exploration algorithm that selects the next RAG configuration to evaluate; RAG-IR, a workload abstraction for diverse RAG algorithms; and RAG-CM, a performance model that predicts the optimal deployment and serving performance on the given hardware. Together, these components allow RAG-Stack to search the joint algorithm-system configuration space without deploying every candidate and to transfer an existing Pareto frontier to a new serving system. Given the same number of optimization iterations across diverse datasets, the Pareto frontiers found by RAG-Stack cover 52.5% to 153.2% more of the normalized quality-performance space than those found by state-of-the-art configuration-search methods evaluated over the same RAG design space.

</details>


### [218] [Evaluating LLMs in Database Scenarios: A Lifecycle Benchmark for Assessing Their Potential in Core Database Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03794)
*Shunfan Zheng,Dongsheng Shi,Yue Li,Xin Yi,Linlin Wang,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出首个覆盖数据库全生命周期（设计、实现、运维、调试、维护）的LLM评估基准DBLifeBench，并引入Progressive-Text2SQL任务以解决自然语言与SQL逻辑的认知鸿沟。研究发现通用模型表现均衡，而专用Text-to-SQL模型在非编码阶段存在“灾难性遗忘”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估基准过度聚焦于Text-to-SQL任务，忽视了从初始模式设计到部署后维护的完整数据库生命周期，无法全面捕捉现实世界数据库管理所需的多样化能力。

Method: 1. 构建DBLifeBench：涵盖数据库生命周期的五个关键阶段（设计、实现、操作、调试、维护）。
2. 提出Progressive-Text2SQL：利用结构化推理图模拟人类迭代问题解决过程，弥合模糊自然语言与复杂SQL逻辑间的认知差异。
3. 对各类LLM进行广泛评估。

Result: 通用大模型在各项任务中表现均衡；而专用的Text-to-SQL模型在设计、维护等非编码阶段出现严重的“灾难性遗忘”现象，性能显著下降。

Conclusion: DBLifeBench是迈向评估和构建真正全栈数据库智能的基础性步骤，揭示了当前专用模型在全链路管理能力上的局限性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are transforming database interaction paradigms, evolving from simple query translators to autonomous database administrators (DBAs). However, current evaluation benchmarks remain disproportionately fixated on Text-to-SQL tasks, neglecting the holistic Database Lifecycle-from initial schema design to post-deployment maintenance. This narrow focus fails to capture the diverse capabilities required for real-world database management. To bridge this gap, we introduce DBLifeBench, the first benchmark to evaluate LLMs across five critical lifecycle phases: Design, Implementation, Operation, Debugging, and Maintenance. Furthermore, addressing the cognitive mismatch between ambiguous natural language and complex SQL logic, we propose Progressive-Text2SQL, a novel task utilizing structured reasoning graphs to mimic human iterative problem-solving. Our extensive evaluation reveals a critical insight: while general-purpose models demonstrate balanced performance, specialized Text-to-SQL models suffer from ``catastrophic forgetting'' in non-coding phases like design and maintenance. DBLifeBench serves as a foundational step toward evaluating and building true full-stack database intelligence.

</details>


### [219] [Uplifting the Superpowers of Worst-Case-Optimal Join Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03840)
*Adrián Gómez-Brandón,Aidan Hogan,Gonzalo Navarro*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将最坏情况最优（WCO）连接算法与过滤操作原生结合的方法，通过扩展Ring索引以支持属性图上的高效查询，实验证明该方法优于现有基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图查询语言（如SPARQL和GQL）的核心是基本图模式（BGP），但也包含过滤器（选择条件）。目前这些条件通常通过预处理或后处理处理，效率较低。旨在提升WCO连接算法在处理含过滤条件的复杂BGP时的效率。

Method: 1. 改进WCO连接算法，使其能够原生地整合过滤操作；2. 扩展现有的Ring紧凑索引（该索引能以极小额外空间实现BGP的WCO解析），使其能够处理带有属性过滤的属性图；3. 实现该扩展并进行实验验证。

Result: 扩展后的Ring索引在保持紧凑性的同时，能够有效处理属性图查询。实验结果表明，该方法在性能上优于多种基线系统。

Conclusion: 将过滤操作原生集成到WCO连接算法中是提升图查询效率的有效途径，特别是对于基于紧凑索引（如Ring）的系统，能在保证空间效率的同时显著提升处理能力。

Abstract: Worst-case-optimal (wco) join algorithms have demonstrated their power -- in both theory and practice -- to efficiently solve complex Basic Graph Patterns (BGPs). Modern graph query languages, such as SPARQL and GQL, have BGPs at their core, but also have a wide range of other features, including filters (aka.\ selections). Such conditions are typically handled via pre- or post-filtering, before or after processing the BGPs. In this paper we show how to uplift wco join algorithms so as to incorporate such filtering natively, improving efficiency. We demonstrate the superiority of this approach by extending the \textit{Ring} -- a compact index that provides wco resolution of BGPs within almost no extra space on top of the graph -- so as to handle property graphs using our new techniques while retaining compactness. We implement this extension and experimentally show that it outperforms various baseline systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Knowledge-Geometry Decoupling: Refreshable Pretrained Transfer for Streaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02738)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuhong Chen,Yuxuan Zhu,Guidong Lei,Zhiluohan Guo,Yu Zhao,Kun Wang,Bangyang Hong,Kangle Wu,Yabo Ni,Anxiang Zeng,Cong Fu,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出知识几何解耦（KGD）框架，通过行为多Token预测和参数分离解决推荐系统中预训练模型的知识漂移与迁移冲突问题，在Shopee上线后显著提升GMV和广告收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界推荐系统广泛采用“预训练-迁移”范式，但行为分布漂移导致两个核心问题：1）如何从行为序列中学习有效知识（传统方法易编码无关会话间的虚假关联）；2）如何在预训练模型持续更新的同时，解决预训练知识与任务特定几何结构对共享参数的优化冲突。

Method: 提出知识几何解耦（KGD）方法：1）行为多Token预测（BMTP）：仅保留协作或语义相关的未来Item作为监督信号，避免无关会话间的虚假依赖，提取更干净的行为知识；2）参数解耦架构：将可刷新编码器（存储行为知识）与任务学习者分离；任务学习者通过只读交叉注意力读取编码器状态，并通过锚定校准残差（ACR）写入与预训练嵌入正交的任务特定几何结构，实现知识更新与任务适配的无干扰并行。

Result: 1）在8个公开基准测试中，KGD相比强基线提升4-12%；2）在90天生产流数据上保持优势，而基线无增长；3）已在Shopee全量部署，首页搜索A/B测试显示用户人均GMV提升1.75%，广告收入提升1.53%。

Conclusion: KGD通过解耦行为知识与任务几何结构，有效解决了预训练推荐系统中的知识漂移与迁移冲突问题，具有显著的实用价值和部署可行性。

Abstract: Industrial recommenders increasingly adopt the pretrain-then-transfer paradigm, yet behavioral distribution drift raises two questions: what to learn from behavior sequences, and how to transfer the learned knowledge while the pretrained model is continually refreshed. To resolve them, we propose Knowledge-Geometry Decoupling (KGD). For what to learn, conventional next-token prediction treats adjacency as dependency and may encode spurious transitions across unrelated sessions. We introduce Behavioral Multi-Token Prediction (BMTP) to retain only collaboratively or semantically related future items as supervision, yielding cleaner and more transferable behavioral knowledge. For how to transfer, pretrained knowledge and task-specific geometry impose conflicting optimization demands on shared parameters. To handle it, KGD assigns them to separate parameter sets: a refreshable encoder owns behavioral knowledge, while a task learner reads contextualized encoder states through read-only cross-attention and writes task-specific geometry through Anchored Calibration Residual (ACR) orthogonal to the pretrained embedding. The decoupled ownership enables continual knowledge refresh without task-gradient interference or invalidating downstream adaptation. KGD improves over strong pretrain-transfer baselines by 4-12% on eight public benchmarks and sustains its advantage over a 90-day production stream where baselines show no gains. KGD has been fully deployed in Shopee. In a live A/B test on Shopee Homepage Search, it increases GMV per user by 1.75% and advertising revenue by 1.53%, demonstrating its high practical value. We provide the core implementation of KGD at https://github.com/FuCongResearchSquad/KGD4REC.

</details>


### [221] [Search, Inspect, Fetch: Exploiting Boolean Retrieval for Deep-Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02751)
*Shuai Wang,Haodong Chen,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SIEVE框架，利用字段级布尔检索（BQL）替代传统的全文页面检索，通过过滤、排序、检查结构丰富结果卡并仅获取选定部分，在三个QA数据集上比传统方法更准确且节省20.7-50.6%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究代理采用“搜索-访问”工作流，检索并阅读整个网页，忽略了Web源通过标题、章节、元数据等暴露的可寻址结构。这导致代理无法直接约束检索范围至文档字段，并将无关内容带入上下文，影响效率与准确性。

Method: 引入SIEVE接口，基于字段布尔查询语言（BQL）。流程包括：在文档字段上过滤候选项、对准入集合进行排序、展示富含结构的搜索结果卡片供检查、仅获取用户选定的章节内容。

Result: 在三个QA集合上，SIEVE的准确率均高于最准确的传统Search-Visit配置；同时使用少20.7%-50.6%的tokens。BQL过滤提升了所有测试的排序器性能，且该优势在不同检索器和代理骨干网络中保持一致。

Conclusion: 利用Web源的结构性信息并通过字段级检索和选择性获取，可显著提升深度研究代理的准确性和效率，降低上下文负载。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Existing deep-research agents use a search-visit workflow that retrieves and reads whole pages, without considering the addressable structure that web sources expose through titles, headings, sections, and metadata. This prevents agents from directly constraining retrieval to document fields and often carries irrelevant page content into their context. We introduce SIEVE, a search-inspect-fetch interface driven by fielded Boolean retrieval (BQL). SIEVE filters candidates over document fields, ranks the admitted set, presents structure-rich result cards for inspection, and fetches only selected sections. Across three QA collections, SIEVE achieves higher accuracy than the most accurate conventional Search-Visit configuration on each collection while using 20.7-50.6% fewer tokens. Further analyses show that BQL filtering improves all tested rankers and that the accuracy-context advantage persists across retriever choices and agent backbones. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ielab/skim-search-agent.

</details>


### [222] [Field Aware Agent Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02880)
*Paimon Goulart,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种字段感知的技能检索方法，通过将结构化技能的各个字段独立计算相似度并结合学习权重，显著优于传统的扁平化拼接方法，尤其在大规模技能库中效果更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着终身学习智能体积累的技能库日益庞大，如何从众多技能中准确检索出正确技能成为瓶颈。现有方法通常将技能视为扁平文档（拼接名称、描述等字段），忽略了技能作为多字段对象的自然结构特性，导致检索效率受限。

Method: 将每个技能表示为其独立的组成部分，分别对每个字段计算稀疏和密集相似度，形成张量化的字段感知表示。随后，通过均匀加权或小型可学习MLP（多层感知机）组合这些字段级得分，以优化最终检索结果。

Result: 在SkillRet和SRA-Bench两个基准测试上，保持字段分离提升了混合检索性能。使用字段级分数学习的MLP模型表现最佳且最稳定：在SkillRet上Recall@10达到77.95，在SRA-Bench上达到83.78，均优于对应的拼接基线模型。此外，随着技能库规模增大，该方法的优势更加显著。

Conclusion: 技能本身的表示方式至关重要。仅保留技能文件中已有的自然结构信息，即可显著提升检索性能，特别是在检索难度较大的大规模场景下，字段感知的检索策略具有极高的实用价值。

Abstract: As lifelong learning agents accumulate lifelong growing skill banks, retrieving the correct skill becomes an increasingly important bottleneck. Most current skill retrieval methods treat each skill as one flat document by concatenating fields such as the name, description, and body. However, skills are naturally structured, multi-field objects, where each field provides different information about when and how the skill should be used. In this work, we study whether preserving this structure improves skill retrieval. We represent each skill as its separate components, and compute sparse and dense similarities for each field independently, exposing a naturally tensorized, field-aware representation of the skill bank. We then combine these field-level scores either with uniform weights or with a small learned MLP. Across two different skill retrieval benchmarks, SkillRet and SRA-Bench, we find that keeping fields separate improves hybrid retrieval, and learning over the field-level scores gives the strongest and most consistent results. Our field-aware MLP reaches $77.95$ Recall@10 on SkillRet and $83.78$ Recall@10 on SRA-Bench, outperforming the corresponding concatenated learned baselines. We also find that the advantage grows as the skill bank becomes larger, suggesting that field-aware skill retrieval becomes especially useful in the setting where retrieval is most difficult. Our results show that skill representation itself matters, and that simply preserving the structure already present in skill files can substantially improve retrieval.

</details>


### [223] [Coverage Matters: MarginMerge for Compressing Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02969)
*Ailar Mahdizadeh,Aria Salari,Sohail Rajabi,Shahriar Mirabbasi,Panos Nasiopoulos,Alireza Morsali*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MarginMerge方法，通过保留查询相关覆盖区域而非独立选择显著性补丁，对冻结的多向量视觉文档检索器（如ColPali/ColQwen）进行压缩。该方法在仅保留5%-10%向量的情况下，将存储量减少90%-95%，同时保持97%-99%的nDCG@5性能，且无需重新训练即可迁移至未见数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多向量视觉文档检索器（如ColPali, ColQwen）因存储细粒度补丁嵌入而导致索引庞大及后期交互评分成本高。现有压缩方法往往基于显著性独立选择补丁，未能有效保留对查询相关的多样化文档区域（即MaxSim匹配最强的区域），导致压缩效率与精度平衡不佳。此外，需解释为何密集渲染页面比自然图像更易压缩。

Method: 引入MarginMerge算法，专为冻结的多向量检索器设计。核心步骤包括：1) 选择感知覆盖范围的锚点（coverage-aware anchors）；2) 对文档补丁进行聚类；3) 使用轻量级共享网络为每个聚类合成一个代表性向量。压缩仅在索引阶段执行一次，检索时仍使用标准的MaxSim接口。

Result: 在ColQwen2.5和ColPali上的六个数据集中，MarginMerge在5%和10%向量保留率下实现了最高的匹配查询无关平均指标。相比未压缩基准，其平均nDCG@5保持在97%-99%，同时减少90%-95%的存储向量。在5%保留率下，相较于几何合并方法，其在所有六个ColQwen2.5数据集上的排名翻转（ranking flips）平均减少了约41%。模型具备零样本迁移能力，无需重训练即可适应未见数据集和不同的保留比率。

Conclusion: MarginMerge通过关注“查询相关覆盖范围”而非单纯的“显著性”，实现了对多向量视觉检索器的高效压缩。该方法在大幅降低存储成本和计算开销的同时，几乎无损地保留了检索精度，并展现出良好的泛化能力和实用性，证明了保留多样化文档区域对于有效压缩的重要性。

Abstract: Multi-vector visual document retrievers such as ColPali and ColQwen achieve strong retrieval by storing fine-grained patch embeddings, but this produces large indexes and costly late-interaction scoring. We argue that effective compression should preserve query-relevant coverage, meaning the diverse document regions that may become the strongest MaxSim match across queries, rather than selecting patches independently by salience. This view also explains why dense rendered pages are easier to compress than natural images. We introduce MarginMerge, a compression method for frozen multi-vector retrievers. It selects coverage-aware anchors, clusters document patches, and uses a lightweight shared network to synthesize one representative per cluster. Compression is performed once during indexing, while retrieval keeps the standard MaxSim interface. Across six datasets on both ColQwen2.5 and ColPali, MarginMerge achieves the highest matched query-agnostic average at 5% and 10% vector retention. Compared with the uncompressed index using the same backbone, it preserves between 97% and 99% of average nDCG@5 while reducing stored document vectors by between 90% and 95%. At 5% retention, it also reduces ranking flips relative to geometric merging on all six ColQwen2.5 datasets by approximately 41% on average. The same model transfers to unseen datasets and retention ratios without retraining.

</details>


### [224] [Position Bias Undermines Preference Consistency in Listwise LLM-Based Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03091)
*Ethan Bito,Yongli Ren,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）作为推荐系统排序器时，因候选项输入顺序不同导致的“位置偏差”问题。通过提出新的评估框架衡量成对偏好不稳定性和全局一致性，实验发现提高相关性或平衡曝光度并不能解决LLM排序结果的不稳定性，表明仅减少边缘曝光偏斜不足以保证排序函数的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在推荐系统中展现出潜力，但其可靠性在候选项排列等价的情况下尚不明确。由于推荐候选项本质上是无序集合，理想的排序器不应受序列化顺序的影响，但现有的Decoder-only LLM排序器往往存在输入顺序影响评分和排名的现象。

Method: 作者将等效候选项排列下产生的排名视为诱导偏好系统的观察值，而非仅测量最终排名列表的变化。引入了一个评估框架，从三个层面量化候选项顺序敏感性：成对偏好不稳定性、全局偏好不一致性以及列表级输出一致性。并在多个LLM、数据集和列表长度上进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验结果显示，上述三种一致性指标之间紧密相关，但它们与推荐有效性及边缘位置曝光偏斜之间存在差异。即使提高了相关性或平滑了各位置的曝光分布，也无法恢复稳定的成对偏好、全局一致的偏好结构或一致的排名输出。

Conclusion: 减少边缘曝光偏斜对于建立基于LLM的重新排序中的排名函数有效性是不足的。LLM排序器存在固有的顺序依赖性问题，需要更深入的机制来解决这一不可靠性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as promising listwise rerankers for recommender systems, but their reliability under equivalent candidate permutations remains unclear. Since recommendation candidates form an unordered set, a reranker should not depend on the arbitrary order used to serialize them. However, decoder-only LLM rerankers can allow input order to affect model scores, pairwise preferences, and rankings. We study how position bias affects the ranking process induced by LLM-based rerankers. Instead of measuring only changes in final ranked lists, we treat rankings produced under equivalent candidate permutations as observations of an induced preference system. We introduce an evaluation framework measuring pairwise preference instability, global preference inconsistency, and listwise output consistency. This framework characterizes candidate-order sensitivity at the pairwise, global, and output levels. Experiments across multiple LLMs, datasets, and list lengths show that these consistency measures are closely aligned, but can diverge from recommendation effectiveness and marginal position-exposure bias. Improving relevance or flattening exposure across positions does not necessarily restore stable pairwise preferences, globally coherent preference structures, or consistent ranked outputs. These results show that reducing marginal exposure skew is insufficient to establish ranking-function validity in LLM-based reranking. Code is available at https://github.com/ejbito/InvariRank .

</details>


### [225] [Attacking and Defending Multi-Agent Collaborative Filtering Systems Through Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03272)
*Anjun Hu,Hanting Xie,Saranya Govindan,Jas Kandola,Kurt Cutajar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究将多智能体系统（MAS）中的攻击与防御方法适配至基于大语言模型的协同过滤（CF）系统，通过AgentCF框架系统评估了“候选数量”和“目录集中度”两个连通性维度对系统脆弱性的影响，揭示了角色不对称性、非单调时间动态及不同攻击目标的差异模式，并探索了流行病启发式指标在鲁棒性评估中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协同过滤系统因数据驱动特性和多智能体交互而继承双重脆弱性。理解连通性如何调节这些系统的脆弱性，有助于开发更稳健的推荐流水线。

Method: 在AgentCF框架下，从两个轴（每个回合每用户的候选物品数量、跨用户的项目目录重叠度）刻画CF连通性，并将MAS文献中的攻击和防御方法适配到基于代理的CF设置中进行系统性评估。

Result: 1. 证实了MAS启发的攻击和防御在代理式CF领域具有部分可迁移性；2. 揭示了连通性两方面特征如何塑造攻防结果，包括用户与项目代理间的角色不对称性、攻击效能的非单调时间动态，以及传播与提取攻击目标的不同模式；3. 评估了流行病启发的静态指标在按预期攻击结果排名CF配置中的适用性，为成本高效的鲁棒性评估提供了可能。

Conclusion: 研究不仅验证了现有MAS攻防方法在CF领域的部分有效性，还深入阐明了连通性结构对系统安全性的具体影响机制，并为未来构建更具鲁棒性的多智能体推荐系统提供了理论依据和实践工具（代码已开源）。

Abstract: Multi-agent collaborative filtering (CF) systems coordinate autonomous LLM-powered user and item agents through natural-language interaction to refine preferences and generate recommendations. These systems inherit vulnerabilities from both their data-driven nature and their multi-agent interactions, which manifest in distinct ways. Understanding how connectivity modulates vulnerability in these systems could facilitate the development of more robust recommendation pipelines.
  In this work, we adapt attacks and defenses from the general multi-agent systems (MAS) literature to the agent-based CF setting, evaluating them under systematically varied connectivity in the AgentCF framework, where CF connectivity is characterized along two axes: (i) candidate count (the number of item candidates per turn per user, measuring user-side interaction density) and (ii) catalog concentration (the degree of item catalog overlap across users).
  Our contributions include: (1) Adaptation: we reproduce MAS-inspired attacks and defenses in the agentic CF domain, confirming partial transferability of original observations. (2) Characterization: we characterize how the two aspects of connectivity shape attack and defense outcomes, revealing role asymmetries between user and item agents, non-monotonic temporal dynamics in attack efficacy, and divergent patterns across dissemination and extraction attack goals. Additionally, as an exploratory extension, we assess the applicability of epidemic-inspired static metrics in ranking CF configurations by expected attack outcome, potentially enabling cost-efficient robustness assessment. Implementation is available at https://github.com/anjunhu/ConnACF

</details>


### [226] [LLM-Derived Priors for Thompson Sampling in Cold-Start Comment Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03382)
*Eugene Lee,Oseong Choi,Byungsoo Kang,Taeyeong Jang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）从用户评论文本中提取语义信号，构建贝叶斯先验以解决 Thompson Sampling 在冷启动阶段因交互数据稀疏导致的性能瓶颈。通过在性别-年龄细分群体中独立更新后验，并在真实 A/B/C 测试中对比均匀先验、性别先验和内容先验，证实 LLM 衍生先验在少量交互证据积累后能显著提升推荐效果，揭示了其在文本丰富型带反馈推荐中的实用价值及部署权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机算法（特别是 Thompson Sampling）虽能适应在线反馈，但在面对新引入的“手臂”（如用户生成的文本评论）时，由于缺乏历史交互数据，常面临冷启动限制。需要利用文本内容的语义信息来弥补早期反馈的不足。

Method: 1. 使用大语言模型（LLM）从候选文本评论中提取语义信号，将其转化为信息丰富的贝叶斯先验，为 Thompson Sampling 提供冷启动支持。
2. 针对聚合层面的响应模式差异，按性别-年龄细分群体分别维护和更新后验概率。
3. 设计两种基于 LLM 的先验：性别先验（Gender Prior，捕捉人口统计学亲和力线索）和内容先验（Content Prior，捕捉标题特定的身份线索）。
4. 在真实世界的在线 A/B/C 测试中，将这两种设计与均匀先进行对比评估。

Result: 1. LLM 衍生先验在反馈稀疏 regimes 中最为有益，且在积累少量交互证据后收益最大。
2. 不同的先验设计导致漏斗层级的不同效应。
3. 点击导向的对齐度在性别先验中最强。
4. 治疗效果在不同人口统计细分群体中存在显著差异。

Conclusion: LLM 衍生的先验可作为文本丰富型带反馈推荐的实用冷启动机制，但其部署需考虑对齐方式和人口统计异质性带来的权衡。

Abstract: Multi-armed bandit algorithms, especially Thompson sampling, are widely used in online recommendation. Despite their ability to adapt from online feedback, these methods often suffer from cold-start limitations when newly introduced arms have little or no interaction history. In our setting, the candidate arms are user-generated textual comments, whose semantic content can reveal a title's appeal before sufficient interaction feedback is available. We therefore use large language models (LLMs) to extract semantic signals from comment text and convert them into informative Bayesian priors that warm-start Thompson sampling under sparse early-stage feedback. To account for aggregate segment-level differences in response patterns, we maintain and update posteriors separately for each gender-age segment. In a real-world online A/B/C test, we compare a uniform prior with two LLM-based designs: a Gender Prior for demographic-affinity cues and a Content Prior for title-specific identity cues. The results show that LLM-based priors are most beneficial in sparse-feedback regimes -- with the largest gains emerging once a small amount of interaction evidence has accumulated -- and that prior design leads to distinct funnel-level effects. We further analyze prior-reward alignment and demographic heterogeneity, finding that click-oriented alignment is strongest for the Gender Prior and that treatment effects vary substantially across demographic segments. These findings suggest that LLM-derived priors can serve as a practical warm-start mechanism for text-rich bandit recommendation, while also revealing deployment trade-offs.

</details>


### [227] [Training Documents Reranker with Search Rubrics for Deep Research Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03527)
*Wenhan Liu,Yu Lu,Qiaolin Xia,Hui Xu,Tong Zhao,Jian Xi,Yutao Zhu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi,Hao Wang,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RubricRanker，通过LLM生成的层级化“检索导向准则”来指导文档重排序，解决现有检索器选出的文档集无法满足深度研究代理复杂需求（如多样性、权威性）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统仅基于相关性匹配选择文档，导致选出的top-k文档集合可能无法同时满足深度研究查询对多样性、简洁性和权威性的复杂信息需求。

Method: 1. 利用强大LLM合成层级化的“搜索导向准则”(Search-oriented rubrics)，明确定义高质量文档集的要求；2. 训练文档重排序模型RubricRanker；3. 采用两阶段训练框架：准则引导的监督微调(SFT)和基于准则的强化学习(RL)。

Result: 在四个深度研究基准测试中，RubricRanker比最强基线高出2.6分，并在五个RAG基准测试中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 引入显式的搜索准则并辅以两阶段训练策略，能有效提升检索结果质量，更好地支持深度研究代理生成高质量答案。

Abstract: Retrieval systems help deep research agents generate high-quality answers by providing relevant documents. However, existing retrievers typically select documents through relevance matching, while individually well-matched top-$k$ documents may not form a \textit{set} that satisfies the complex information needs of an agent query (\eg, diverse, concise and authoritative documents). In this paper, we propose search-oriented rubrics that \textit{explicitly} define the requirements that high-quality document sets should satisfy for each agent query. Our search rubrics are organized into a hierarchical structure and synthesized using a powerful LLM. Based on these search rubrics, we further train a document reranker \textbf{RubricRanker} to select a high-quality subset from retrieved documents. We design a two-stage training framework that consists of rubrics-guided supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RubricRanker outperforms the strongest baseline by 2.6 points on four deep research benchmarks and generalizes well to five RAG benchmarks.

</details>


### [228] [Conditionally Identifiable Latent-Environment Modeling for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03647)
*Qianqian Wang,Wenwu Gong,Yunshan Li,Zhenqing Wu,Ruili Wang,Lili Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CILER模型，通过条件可识别性解决分布外(OOD)推荐中的偏好偏移问题，在特征、时间和地理偏移下显著提升排名指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD推荐方法虽能从日志交互中推断潜在状态，但潜在环境的统计意义及其对偏好的影响仍未被充分确定，导致模型鲁棒性不足。

Method: 构建条件可识别风险感知推荐(CI-RR)框架，提出CILER算法：使用用户条件的指数族建模潜在环境，用特征索引多项式指定其如何改变偏好，并通过边际化推断的环境分布来预测物品概率。

Result: 在充分变异、正确设定和解码器正则性条件下，CILER能识别出与环境敏感相关的表示；理论上界定了由环境推断误差导致的部署超额对数风险；在三个数据集上的实验表明，CILER在共享支持域内的特征、时间及地理偏移下，十二项OOD排名指标均有所提升。

Conclusion: CILER通过明确建模潜在环境及其对偏好的影响，有效解决了OOD推荐中的不确定性问题，证明了条件可识别性在提升推荐系统泛化能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) recommendation is vulnerable to preference shifts induced by a latent environment. Existing methods can infer latent states from logged interactions, yet the statistical meaning of the latent environment and its effect on preference remain underdetermined. We formulate this task as conditionally identifiable risk-aware recommendation (CI-RR) and propose Conditionally Identifiable Latent-Environment Recommendation (CILER). CILER uses a user-conditioned exponential family to model the latent environment and a feature-indexed polynomial to specify how it changes preference. It predicts by marginalizing item probabilities over the inferred environment distribution. Under sufficient variation, correct specification, and decoder regularity, CILER identifies the environment-sensitive representation up to the stated equivalence class. We further bound excess deployment log-risk by environment-inference error. Controlled studies test the observable consequences of sufficient variation and model specification. Experiments on three datasets show that CILER improves all twelve OOD ranking metrics under feature, temporal, and geographical shifts within shared support.

</details>


### [229] [SITA: Semantic Interest Tokens for Target-Aware Compression in Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03692)
*Rui Zhou,Bo Chen,Qinglin Jia,Jiezhou Ji,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SITA框架，通过语义量化将用户行为压缩为具有语义结构的表示，在推理时根据目标物品自适应聚合兴趣，兼顾了目标感知建模与高效可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长序列推荐方法存在两难困境：动态检索方法虽能实现目标感知但推理成本高；整体压缩方法效率高但缺乏目标特异性。旨在解决如何在保持压缩表示的高效性和可扩展性的同时，实现目标感知的建模问题。

Method: 提出SITA（Target-aware Compression Framework），利用并行语义量化学习语义标识符，将压缩的兴趣组织成语义结构。在推理阶段，基于目标物品的语义标识符，自适应地聚合对应的结构化兴趣以构建目标特定的用户表示。

Result: 在公开数据集和大规模工业数据集上的实验表明，SITA在保持强可扩展性的同时，持续优于代表性基线模型。

Conclusion: SITA成功平衡了目标感知建模与计算效率，展现了在实际推荐系统中应用的巨大潜力。

Abstract: As user behavior histories continue to grow on modern Internet platforms, effectively modeling long behavior sequences has become crucial for predicting user interests in candidate items. Existing methods have evolved along two directions. One line dynamically retrieves target-relevant behaviors from long histories, enabling target-aware modeling but requiring target-dependent computation during inference. The other line compresses entire behavior sequences into compact user representations, achieving high efficiency and scalability but sacrificing target-specific adaptation due to target-independent encoding. The key challenge is therefore to enable target-aware modeling while preserving the efficiency and scalability of compressed user representations. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{SITA}, a target-aware compression framework for long-sequence recommendation. SITA enables target-aware compression by organizing compressed interests into semantic structures through semantic identifiers learned via parallel semantic quantization. Conditioned on the semantic identifier of the target item, SITA adaptively aggregates the corresponding structured interests to construct the target-specific user representation. Extensive experiments on public datasets and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that SITA consistently outperforms representative baselines while maintaining strong scalability, highlighting its strong potential for real-world recommender systems.

</details>


### [230] [LegalPincite: Multi-level Legal Information Retrieval Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03756)
*Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Henrik Palmer Olsen,Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一个基于欧盟法院（CJEU）判决的大规模法律信息检索数据集，解决现有数据存在查询文本泄露及语料库不完整的问题，支持案例和段落级别的多粒度检索评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共法律IR数据集缺乏段落级引用标注，且包含公开数据集中的查询文本泄露问题以及排除非引用/被引用段落的设定，导致检索环境不切实际且性能虚高。

Method: 构建基于欧盟法院（CJEU）判决的数据集，包含：(i) 去除引用信息的掩码案例/段落查询；(ii) 包含所有段落的完整语料库；(iii) 经部分人类专家验证的案例和段落级真实引用标注。

Result: 提供了一个支持案例到案例、段落到案例及段落到段落多粒度检索的法律IR数据集，可用于法律IR方法的开发与严格评估。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过消除数据泄露和确保语料库完整性，为法律信息检索研究提供了更真实、严谨的评估基准。

Abstract: A common task in legal Information Retrieval (IR) is to find relevant legal sources from case-law collections. While legal practice often requires pinpoint citations (pincites) to specific case paragraphs, most existing public legal IR datasets lack paragraph-level citation annotations. Yet, publicly available datasets with such information contain data leakage in the query text and exclude paragraphs that are neither citing nor cited from the corpora, creating an unrealistic and oversimplified retrieval setting, potentially leading to inflated performance. To address these limitations, we contribute a large-scale legal IR dataset constructed from Court of Justice of the European Union (CJEU) judgments. The dataset contains: (i) masked case/paragraph queries, with removed citation information; (ii) a corpus that includes all paragraphs; and (iii) case- and paragraph-level ground-truth citations, with partial human expert validation. Our dataset supports both the development and rigorous evaluation of legal IR methods, at multiple query-document levels (case-to-case, paragraph-to-case, and paragraph-to-paragraph retrieval). Link to dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/theresiavr/legalpincite

</details>


### [231] [ATLAS: Learning to Recommend Across Unseen Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03899)
*Pervez Shaik,Prosenjit Biswas,Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kolla,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS框架，通过多源异构领域学习共享的领域不变表示，实现零样本推荐，无需目标域适配或大语言模型预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统局限于特定领域，跨域迁移通常需重新训练、目标域适配或依赖大语言模型；旨在探索仅从多个异构领域学习的知识能否泛化至完全未见领域。

Method: 引入ATLAS框架，结合Gromov-Wasserstein对齐（保留用户间关系）、对抗性目标（使物品表示跨域不可区分）和残差向量量化（RVQ）码本（压缩嵌入以捕捉层级交互模式并抑制领域特异性变异）。

Result: 在五个Amazon领域训练并直接应用于十个未见领域，ATLAS在大多数未见领域上优于现有基线（序列、图、跨域、量化及LLM方法），HitRate平均相对提升24%；发现源领域多样性显著促进零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 推荐领域泛化是零样本推荐的有前景范式，ATLAS成功实现了无需适配的跨域零样本推荐。

Abstract: Recommender systems remain domain-bound: a model trained on one interaction environment typically requires retraining or target-domain adaptation before it can operate on a new catalogue. A recommender trained on movies cannot be directly deployed to recommend groceries or video games. Existing approaches mitigate this by transferring restricted forms of recommendation knowledge, adapting to the target domain, or leveraging large language models (LLMs) for transferable representations. We instead ask whether recommendation-specific knowledge learned solely from multiple heterogeneous domains can generalize to entirely unseen domains without target-domain adaptation or language-model pretraining. We introduce ATLAS, a multi-source recommendation domain generalization framework that learns a shared, domain-invariant user-item representation from disjoint source domains, enabling zero-shot recommendation on unseen domains. ATLAS combines a Gromov-Wasserstein alignment that preserves how users relate to one another across domains, an adversarial objective that makes item representations indistinguishable across domains, and residual vector quantization (RVQ) codebooks that compress user and item embeddings into a discrete latent space, capturing hierarchical interaction patterns while suppressing domain-specific variation. Trained on five Amazon domains and applied directly to ten unseen domains, ATLAS outperforms state-of-the-art sequential, graph-based, cross-domain, quantization-based, and LLM-based baselines on most unseen domains, with an average relative gain in HitRate of 24%. Ablations and representation analyses validate each component, and we identify a pronounced source-domain diversity effect: increasing source heterogeneity substantially improves zero-shot transfer. ATLAS establishes recommendation domain generalization as a promising paradigm for zero-shot recommendation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ISEE系统，通过交互式语义丰富解决LLM代理在数据任务中因字段描述模糊导致的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在数据相关任务中的表现严重依赖数据语义的清晰度与完整性，但实际应用中许多字段描述存在歧义或不完整，且关键领域知识通常未公开记录，限制了实体链接等下游任务的性能。

Method: 引入交互语义丰富系统（ISEE），对数据字段描述进行质量评分，收集领域知识，并与用户协作共同丰富语义。

Result: 通过用户研究、自动化模拟、定量评估和案例研究证明，ISEE显著降低了认知负荷，提高了描述质量，并增强了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ISEE有效弥合了数据语义缺失与LLM代理需求之间的差距，提升了数据任务的整体效能。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [233] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种受生物可塑性启发的自组织数字电路架构，利用拓扑掩码Transformer在布尔搜索空间中动态配置逻辑门查找表（LUT），实现了从初始组装到故障后快速重路由的自适应功能维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经典计算容错依赖静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统通过动态重组维持功能。旨在借鉴生物自适应可塑性原理，解决数字硬件在面对永久性硬件故障和软错误时的灵活性与鲁棒性问题。

Method: 将功能逻辑的生成与维护框架化为图上的元学习问题。采用拓扑掩码Transformer架构来配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT）。该方法扩展了神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，在简并布尔搜索空间中进行导航以满足计算任务，而非再生固定目标状态。

Result: 1. 能够从零开始自组装功能性电路；2. 能迅速绕过永久性且之前未见过的硬件故障重新路由逻辑；3. 对于软错误，在远超训练条件的损伤规模下实现近乎完美的恢复（准确率>99.99%）；4. 具有跨电路规模的泛化能力，在比训练时宽得多的图上准确率反而提升。

Conclusion: 该工作成功 bridging 了生物自组织原则与数字硬件的实际应用领域，证明了基于图的元学习方法在构建具有高度适应性和容错能力的数字电路方面的有效性。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [234] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Persona Anchoring与过滤温度缩放（FTS）框架，通过双阶段生成过程解决大语言模型中的“人工蜂群思维”效应，显著降低语义收敛性，提升AI输出的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型（LLMs）中存在的“人工蜂群思维”效应，即模型在开放性问题回答上趋向于狭窄、同质化的共识，导致语义坍塌和高响应相似性，限制了AI的多样性。

Method: 提出结合元角色锚定与过滤温度缩放（FTS）的新缓解框架。采用两阶段生成过程：首先提示模型自我选择独特的个性以锚定起点；其次应用双阶段采样筛，先使用Top-p过滤保持语法有效性，再对幸存者进行极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）以探索更广泛的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集及约20B参数的开源模型上评估，平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85显著降至约0.65。多数问题的相似度低于0.7阈值，有效缩小了人工模式坍塌与人类类型多样性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该方案能有效减少语义收敛，实现更多样化和创造性的AI部署，并作为开源框架提供。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [235] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PULSE语言，基于对象-过程方法论，将知识图谱工程中的操作角色、状态变更及约束集中在单一类型化运行时环境中，通过形式化验证（Lean 4）确保安全性，并在实际数据集（NOAA IBTrACS）中验证了其与传统工具的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决知识图谱工程中接受的状态、观察结果、约束、过程和假设场景分散在不同工件中，导致执行契约外部化且难以管理的问题。

Method: 1. 设计PULSE语言：受对象-过程方法论启发，本地化四种操作角色及其写入效应。
2. 形式化验证：建立核心演算，证明效果隔离引理和六个安全属性；使用Lean 4检查内核类比（位置、证据、时钟、监视器、原子性等）。
3. 实现与测试：进行88项测试、3,534次有界检查和32个Lean/Python运行时-内核案例；生成37,440条时间轨迹进行变异测试。
4. 实证评估：在完整的NOAA IBTrACS（自1980年以来的飓风数据子集）上，与GEOS工具和事件扫描算法对比，覆盖1,476,290个过渡带对。

Result: 1. PULSE成功实现了合同本地化、安全论证和测试片段的轨迹一致性。
2. 在大规模真实世界数据（NOAA IBTrACS）上，PULSE与现有标准工具（GEOS）高度一致。
3. 能够区分十个单字段变异体，证明其检测能力。
4. 生成的GeoSPARQL、SOSA和SHACL视图保持兼容。

Conclusion: 研究支持了合同本地化、安全论证和轨迹一致性的有效性，证明了PULSE在特定片段上的可靠性；但语言优越性和可用性不在本次评估范围内。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events. Project-specific GeoSPARQL probes measure interface coverage. Overall, the results support contract localization, safety arguments, and trace parity for the tested fragment; language superiority and usability remain outside the evaluation.

</details>


### [236] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HyperAgent框架，通过构建工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）指导动态规划与执行，解决LLM智能体在复杂任务中工具使用效率低和可靠性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的工具使用智能体依赖文本描述推断工具组合，导致复杂任务中探索效率低下、执行不可靠，且难以应对实时执行环境的变化及隐式推理的局限性。

Method: 1. 在模式层面建模工具关系，构建有向“工具-模式超图”，将工具表示为从输入模式节点到输出模式节点的超边；2. 提出HyperAgent框架，首先提取任务相关工具上下文图以构建感知模式的Task DAG；3. 执行阶段通过面向缺陷的扩展构建状态条件工具支持图，根据当前智能体状态识别未满足需求并检索生产者工具。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，相比现有智能体基线，HyperAgent提高了任务完成性能，同时减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数及Token消耗。

Conclusion: 基于模式层面的超图结构能有效指导LLM智能体的动态规划与执行，显著提升工具使用的效率和可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [237] [On the missing data layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02949)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对拉丁美洲AI基础设施中缺失的数据集层问题，提出“DataHub”方案。该方案通过任务优先的本体论组织数据（/<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>），旨在解决数据集分散难寻和总量不足两大难题，提供从发现、元数据到贡献、许可及复用的完整机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺乏AI基础设施的基础层，特别是数据集层和基准层。当前该地区AI数据集存在但分布零散，缺乏统一索引；即使建立完美索引，现有数据总量也远不足以支持前沿AI开发需求。

Method: 提出DataHub，一种以任务为导向的数据基础设施。其核心是通过本体论结构（/<任务?>/<领域?>/<语言?>）组织数据，并集成数据集发现、元数据管理、贡献机制、许可管理及复用功能。

Result: 构建了一个能够系统性解决数据集发现难和供给少问题的平台框架，为拉丁美洲AI发展提供标准化的数据基础设施支持。

Conclusion: DataHub通过结构化的数据组织和完善的生态机制，填补了拉丁美洲在AI数据集层面的基础设施空白，促进了数据的可发现性、可用性和可持续供应。

Abstract: Latin America is missing two foundational layers of AI infrastructure: the dataset layer and the benchmark layer. This paper targets the dataset layer. The dataset layer faces two compounding problems: discovery and supply. Latin American AI datasets exist but are scattered across platforms with no shared index. Even with perfect indexing, the total volume would remain far below what frontier AI development requires. We propose DataHub: a task-first data infrastructure organized through the ontology /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>, with mechanisms for dataset discovery, metadata, contribution, licensing, and reuse.

</details>


### [238] [Explainable AI for the EU Right to Explanation: A Systematic Review of the Law-XAI Translation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02699)
*Benjamin Fresz,Elena Dubovitskaya,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对2024年以来XAI文献的系统综述，指出当前研究在结合欧盟《通用数据保护条例》(GDPR)和《人工智能法案》(AIA)法律要求方面存在严重跨学科整合缺口。文章揭示了三个主要问题模式，提出了“收件人/目的框架”及操作蓝图，并呼吁解决六大开放性问题，以避免“解释权”沦为无法技术落地的形式义务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管欧盟法律赋予个体对自动化决策的“解释权”，但可解释人工智能（XAI）如何在实践中满足这一权利尚不明确，直接影响个人挑战影响其生活之自动化决策的能力。随着2024年7月AIA最终版本发布及第86条的加入，亟需评估最新法规下的技术响应情况。

Method: 对2024年及以后发表的关于欧盟语境下XAI的文献进行系统综述。从2643条初始记录中筛选出57篇全文，其中仅19篇展示了法律与技术视角的实质性整合。分析这些文献在法律基础识别、判例引用及概念区分上的表现。

Result: 发现当前文献存在三大问题模式：1）大多数错误识别GDPR的法律依据；2）极少涉及欧洲法院Dun & Bradstreet判决（可能因出版时机）；3）常混淆解释的形式（由收件人决定）与内容（由法律目的决定）。基于此，构建了“收件人/目的框架”。

Conclusion: 若缺乏进一步进展，“解释权”风险成为一项没有技术可实现合规路径的形式义务。为此，文章提出了四阶段操作蓝图并确定了六个具体的开放研究问题，以促进技术与法律的深度融合。

Abstract: When algorithms make or influence consequential decisions---about loan eligibility, hiring, or healthcare---EU law grants affected individuals a Right to Explanation. Yet whether (and how) Explainable AI (XAI) can satisfy this right in practice remains poorly understood, with direct implications for individuals' ability to contest automated decisions that affect their lives. This paper presents a systematic literature review of XAI in the context of the EU Right to Explanation, with particular focus on Art. 15(1)(h) GDPR, Art. 86 AI Act (AIA), and related instruments. We consider papers published from 2024 onwards, as the final version of the AIA was published in July 2024---with Art. 86 being added late. From 2643 initial records identified by a deliberately broad search, we review 57 full texts, of which only 19 papers demonstrate substantive integration of both legal and technical perspectives, showing gaps in the interdisciplinary synthesis of the current regulatory framework. We document three problematic patterns across the corpus: Most misidentify the GDPR legal basis; few engage with the CJEU's Dun & Bradstreet judgment (likely due to publication timing); and the distinction between explanation form (governed by addressee) and content (governed by legal purpose) is often conflated. We conceptualize this as the Addressee/Purpose Framework, propose a four-phase blueprint for operationalization, and identify six concrete open research questions. Without further progress, the Right to Explanation risks remaining a formal obligation without a technically realizable path to compliance.

</details>


### [239] [Predictive Set Theory: A Generative Framework for Cognitive Architecture with Operationalized Core Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02704)
*Yiyang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测集理论（PST），一种基于集合论公理的形式化生成框架，旨在解决预测处理理论和贝叶斯认知科学在定义“预测”结构、误差响应机制及对象离散化方面的不足。PST通过身份函数、状态刷新和三种参考链推导核心认知功能，为系统在不确定性和不可逆风险下保持内部一致性提供设计规范，并解决了罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性等经典问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测处理理论缺乏对“预测”结构、标准化误差响应及跨更新一致性的操作性定义；贝叶斯认知科学虽涵盖概率信念更新，但预设了封闭假设空间，且无法生成性地解释概率分布的对象如何成为离散、可识别的指称物。

Method: 从第一性原理出发构建预测集理论（PST），将认知锚定于最小操作集：传感器形式化为恒等函数、集合论状态刷新，以及三种基本参考链形式（参考、反参考、半参考）。在此基础上严格推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率和有限视界概率规划等核心认知功能。

Result: PST不仅重构了认知架构，还作为任何需在信息不全和风险不可逆条件下维持内部一致性的系统的设计规范。该框架提供了对罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性的认知地位、负反馈的基础以及电影剪辑理解等经典问题的新颖解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文旨在通过公开学术记录确立预测集理论（PST）框架的原创性和完整性，证明其作为认知架构基础形式化框架的有效性。

Abstract: Predictive processing theories portray the brain as a hierarchical prediction engine that minimizes prediction error, yet they lack operational definitions for the structure of a "prediction," the standardized response to a prediction error, and the mechanism that maintains consistency across successive updates. Bayesian cognitive science attempts to subsume all uncertainty under probabilistic belief updating, but it presupposes a closed hypothesis space and provides no generative account of how the objects over which probabilities are distributed become discrete, identifiable referents in the first place. This paper introduces Predictive Set Theory (PST), a formal generative framework that reconstructs cognitive architecture from first principles. PST anchors cognition in a minimal set of operations---a sensor formalized as an identity function, set-theoretic state refresh, and three fundamental forms of reference chains (reference, counter-reference, and semi-reference)---and rigorously derives core cognitive functions including state sequences, demand, comparison, efficiency, and finite-horizon probabilistic planning. Rather than modeling neural mechanisms, PST constitutes a design specification for any system that must maintain internal consistency while acting under incomplete information and irreversible risk. The framework offers novel resolutions to classical problems such as Russell's paradox, the cognitive status of Gödelian incompleteness, the grounding of negative feedback, and the comprehension of film editing. The primary purpose of this paper is to establish, through the public academic record, the originality and completeness of the Predictive Set Theory framework.

</details>


### [240] [Towards a new paradigm of scientific discovery with socialized artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02775)
*Xinjie Yao,Xingxin Xu,Xiyuan Gao,Zhoupeng Guo,Kunlong Yang,Dengyu Zhao,Siqi Zhao,Zhihe Fan,Yichen Dong,Xin Li,Jiekang Feng,Jiahe Wu,Sen Wang,Beiming Yu,Kejia Zhao,Ruipu Zhao,Jiaqi Zhou,Heyang Li,Jianjun Chen,Anbo Dai,Xin Liu,Zhengtao Yu,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BLAZE范式，将AI作为科学发现的组织基础设施，通过连接知识、集体推理、实证验证和人类判断，实现可追溯、可重复且累积的社会化科学智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现面临新前沿：挑战不再仅是产生更多信息，而是如何将扩展的知识、推理和证据组织成连贯的发现过程。

Method: 引入“文献、代理与零间隙实验桥接”（BLAZE）范式，构建社会化科学智能框架，将AI视为组织基础设施而非孤立任务的助手，整合持续知识、集体推理、实证验证和人类判断于连续的研究生命周期中。

Result: BLAZE通过将人类和机器组织在共享的科学过程中，使科学发现更具可追溯性、可重复性和累积性，同时保留人类的创造力、判断力和责任感。

Conclusion: 科学智能源于知识、假设、实验和集体验证之间的持续互动，而非仅靠计算；BLAZE旨在扩展集体科学探究的规模、深度和连续性，为下一个科学时代奠定基础，而非取代人类发现。

Abstract: Scientific discovery has advanced through successive transformations in the organization of knowledge. Observation and experimentation established the empirical foundations of science. Theory made it possible to derive general principles from particular phenomena. Computation extended inquiry into systems beyond direct observation, while data-intensive methods opened new spaces of pattern and prediction. Science now confronts a different frontier. The central challenge is no longer simply to produce more information, but to organize expanding knowledge, reasoning, and evidence into a coherent process of discovery. Here, we introduce Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation (BLAZE), a paradigm of socialized scientific intelligence. BLAZE conceives AI not as an assistant for isolated research tasks, but as an organizational infrastructure for scientific discovery. It connects persistent knowledge, collective reasoning, empirical validation, and human judgment within a continuous research lifecycle, transforming fragmented activities into a cumulative process of inquiry, criticism, and revision. The central premise of BLAZE is that scientific intelligence does not arise from computation alone. It emerges from the sustained interaction among knowledge, hypotheses, experiments, and collective verification. By organizing humans and machines within a shared scientific process, BLAZE makes discovery more traceable, reproducible, and cumulative while preserving human creativity, judgment, and responsibility. Socialized scientific intelligence may provide a foundation for the next era of science. Its purpose is not to replace human discovery, but to extend the scale, depth, and continuity of collective scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [241] [BAP-SQL: Budget-Aware Observation Planning for Agentic Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02876)
*Chong Peng,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Varun Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BAP-SQL框架，通过预算控制阶段优化Agent在文本到SQL任务中的表现，利用风险估计、SQL重写和运行时屏蔽机制，在有限上下文下显著提升成功率并减少Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用代理（如Agentic Text-to-SQL）中，动作决定后续观测结果。广泛查询可能耗尽上下文和数据库资源后才出现有用证据，且事后压缩无法恢复已省略的行或已消耗的工作，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出BAP-SQL方法，将观测形成视为预算控制阶段：1) 估计查询风险；2) 在必要时重写SQL；3) 将硬限制委托给独立的运行时屏蔽器（runtime shield）。基于4B通用模型、专用FINER-SQL 4B及7B骨干网络进行验证。

Result: 在主要BIRD衍生设置上，相比匹配的SFT方法，BAP-SQL成功率提升3.4/3.6个百分点，同时Token消耗减少4.5/5.0%。性能增益与策略可见的规划和预算敏感型救援机制相关。但随着模型能力和预算增加，收益递减，在最宽松设置下甚至逆转，且未减少数据库工作量。

Conclusion: BAP-SQL通过主动预算控制和动态规划，在资源受限场景下有效提升了Text-to-SQL任务的性能和效率，证明了策略感知的预算分配在Agent系统中的价值，但其优势受限于模型能力和可用预算水平。

Abstract: Tool-using agents do not merely consume observations: their actions determine what arrives next. In agentic text-to-SQL, a broad query can spend context and database work before useful evidence appears, while post-hoc compression cannot recover omitted rows or expended work. We present BAP-SQL, which treats observation formation as a budget-control stage: it estimates query risk, rewrites SQL when useful, and delegates hard limits to an independent runtime shield. Across general 4B, specialized FINER-SQL 4B, and 7B backbones, BAP-SQL improves tight-budget success. On the primary BIRD-derived setting, it gains 3.4/3.6 percentage points over matched SFT while using 4.5/5.0% fewer tokens. Matched retraining and task-level transfer associate the gain with policy-visible planning and budget-sensitive rescue. The benefit attenuates as model capability and budget increase, reverses at the loosest setting, and does not reduce database work.

</details>


### [242] [VeriTrace: Human-Like Temporal Exploration Completes Agentic Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02878)
*Yu-Tung Liu,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VeriTrace多智能体系统，通过完整的调试动作空间（包括信号选择、时间窗口和迭代深度的独立控制）解决大语言模型在Verilog RTL生成中遇到的准确率瓶颈，在VerilogEval-V2基准上实现100% Pass@1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进多智能体系统在标准基准测试上的准确率停滞在~95%，主要原因在于调试动作空间不完整，限制了智能体可检查的信号、查询的时间窗口或两者，导致调试退化为对狭窄电路行为视图的模式匹配，而非基于假设的根本原因分析。

Method: 提出VeriTrace多智能体系统，引入Inspector智能体，赋予其对信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度的独立控制权， termed "Agentic Temporal Exploration"。该机制使智能体能形成关于失败原因的假设，查询波形证据，并迭代完善理解，模拟人类验证工程师的探索过程。

Result: 在VerilogEval-V2基准测试上达到100% Pass@1准确率，成为首个在该基准上实现完美功能正确性的系统。在使用共享Claude Sonnet 4.0主干的情况下，比最强的复现基线高出+5.1%。

Conclusion: 通过赋予智能体完整的调试代理能力（debugging agency），可以弥补最终的性能差距，显著提升自动化Verilog RTL生成的准确性。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promise for automated Verilog RTL generation, yet state-of-the-art multi-agent systems plateau at ~95% accuracy on standard benchmarks. We trace this ceiling to an incomplete debugging action space: existing systems restrict which signals the agent can inspect, which time windows it can query, or both, reducing debugging to pattern matching on a narrow, predetermined view of circuit behavior rather than hypothesis-driven root-cause analysis. We present VeriTrace, a multi-agent system whose Inspector agent operates over a complete debugging action space, with independent control over signal selection, time-window bounds, and iteration depth. This capability, which we term Agentic Temporal Exploration, enables the agent to form hypotheses about failure causes, query the waveform for evidence, and refine its understanding iteratively, mirroring the exploratory process of human verification engineers. VeriTrace achieves 100\% Pass@1 on VerilogEval-V2, the first system to attain perfect functional correctness on this benchmark. On a shared Claude Sonnet 4.0 backbone, VeriTrace outperforms the strongest reproduced baseline by +5.1%, demonstrating that debugging agency closes the final accuracy gap.

</details>


### [243] [Interpreting Black-Box Large Language Models with Sentence-Level Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02879)
*Maryam Rezaee,Pooriya Safaei,Maryam Asgarinezhad,Fatemeh Seyyedsalehi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于能量基模型（EBM）的模型无关、事后句级归因解释器，用于量化提示词句子对大语言模型输出的影响，无需额外API查询即可实现黑盒模型的内部一致性模拟与可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有大型语言模型（LLMs）主要通过封闭API访问，导致部署时缺乏可解释性，难以理解模型决策依据。

Method: 1. 训练能量基模型（EBM）作为代理模型，捕捉LLM在提示与响应间的内部概念一致性；2. 利用EBM构建的能量景观指导轻量级解释器网络的训练；3. 该解释器为独立工具，训练后直接量化提示句子对目标输出的影响力，无需再次调用LLM API；4. 通过跨多样输入的全局训练局部解释器，捕获更广泛的生成模式并缓解实例特定偏差。
